# Skills · D · part 35

978 entries, db-interface-procedure-flow to db-replicas-views. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **db-interface-procedure-flow**: Hướng dẫn tích hợp và điều phối chuỗi Stored Procedures / DB-Interface qua Adapter Pattern, đảm bảo cô lập Database Core, Idempotency và cơ chế bù trừ (Compensation). ([TienDat-Vna/BackSpec](https://github.com/TienDat-Vna/BackSpec/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/db-interface-procedure-flow) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **db-interpro**: Query InterPro for protein family, domain, and functional site annotations. Integrates Pfam, PANTHER, PRINTS, SMART, SUPERFAMILY, and 11 other member databases. Use for protein function prediction, domain architecture analysis, evolutionary classification, and GO term mapping. ([biomaps-infra/blender-opencode](https://github.com/biomaps-infra/blender-opencode/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/db-interpro))
- **db-interview-coach**: Run a database coding interview practice session. Use when starting a DB session, coaching schema Q&A, DDL execution, or query phases. Always use after db-problem-gen, or when the user says "start db session" or "db practice". When the session ends, hand off to db-session-review. ([ericlow/learn-python](https://github.com/ericlow/learn-python/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/db-interview-coach))
- **db-introspect**: Read a live Postgres schema — tables, columns, types, primary keys, indexes, and the foreign-key graph — into a normalized snapshot for planning and debugging. Read-only. Use before changing data access, writing a migration, or reasoning about how tables relate. ([nuttaruj/rolepod-dblab](https://github.com/nuttaruj/rolepod-dblab/tree/HEAD/skills/db-introspect))
- **db-introspector**: Read-only inspection of a live Azure SQL schema BEFORE authoring or applying an Alembic migration — verify what actually exists (tables, columns, nullability, indexes, FKs, row counts, alembic_version) vs. what the SQLAlchemy models and alembic/versions claim; or dump an EXTERNAL read-only database's schema into docs/design/external-schema.md for the AI query tool. Use before the write-migration skill / migration-author agent. Requires a reachable DATABASE_URL (or EXTERNAL_DATABASE_URL) — the dev Azure SQL database, since there is no local database. ([YasserAly13/TAChatbot](https://github.com/YasserAly13/TAChatbot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/db-introspector) · 4 copies)
- **db-investigator**: | ([ethanbytes/Self-Evolving-Skill](https://github.com/ethanbytes/Self-Evolving-Skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/db-investigator) · ★33)
- **db-io**: Designing an I/O object (Store) over a database — transactions, isolation, migrations, and the distinguishable failure branches (locked / disk-full / timeout / unavailable / constraint) that become the component-test failure scenarios. Use when a slice reads/writes a DB (io: db). The Store is a pure pipe (no transformations, not unit-tested); the schema + migrations are its contract. Do NOT use for sync HTTP (http-io), queues (queue-io), or module logic (program-design). Tier-agnostic: decision tables, adapter-branch list, checklist, STOP. ([codemonstersteam/rationaldev-ai-sdlc-skills](https://github.com/codemonstersteam/rationaldev-ai-sdlc-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lib/db-io) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **db-io-tuning**: Cut database IO — cache and working set, read/write amplification, WAL/fsync, checkpoints, bloat, volume IOPS limits. Not plans or indexes. ([MohammadBafkar/polymath](https://github.com/MohammadBafkar/polymath/tree/HEAD/plugins/polymath-database/skills/db-io-tuning))
- **db-isolation-bootstrap**: Wire (or verify) per-service PostgreSQL isolation per uber-m3.md §1 — each service connects to its own PG instance (uberdb-users, uberdb-drivers, uberdb-rides, uberdb-locations, uberdb-payments). Removes cross-service @ManyToOne (becomes plain Long) and any cross-DB native @Query JOIN. Generates the K8s StatefulSet stub for the service's own PG. Critical Rule ([seiftarek158/01-15-Reasons-Why-Uber](https://github.com/seiftarek158/01-15-Reasons-Why-Uber/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/db-isolation-bootstrap) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **db-isolation-levels**: &gt; The four SQL standard isolation levels control which concurrent transaction side-effects are visible, with PostgreSQL implementing them via MVCC snapshots rather than traditional locking. ([Intense-Visions/harness-engineering](https://github.com/Intense-Visions/harness-engineering/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/claude-code/db-isolation-levels) · ★23 · 4 copies)
- **db-isolation-selection**: &gt; Selecting the right isolation level requires matching the workload's correctness requirements against the performance cost and retry complexity of stricter levels. ([Intense-Visions/harness-engineering](https://github.com/Intense-Visions/harness-engineering/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/claude-code/db-isolation-selection) · ★23 · 4 copies)
- **db-jaspar**: Query JASPAR for transcription factor binding site (TFBS) profiles (PWMs/PFMs). Search by TF name, species, or class; scan DNA sequences for TF binding sites; compare matrices; essential for regulatory genomics, motif analysis, and GWAS regulatory variant interpretation. ([biomaps-infra/blender-opencode](https://github.com/biomaps-infra/blender-opencode/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/db-jaspar))
- **db-kegg**: Direct REST API access to KEGG (academic use only). Pathway analysis, gene-pathway mapping, metabolic pathways, drug interactions, ID conversion. For Python workflows with multiple databases, prefer bioservices. Use this for direct HTTP/REST work or KEGG-specific control. ([20minds/sennen-computational-discovery](https://github.com/20minds/sennen-computational-discovery/tree/HEAD/sennen/skills/db-kegg) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **db-kernel-func-skill**: 数据库内核 SQL 内置函数合成（SQLite / PostgreSQL / DuckDB / ClickHouse）。 覆盖各库的函数注册入口与模式、四大开发规范（功能准确/代码集成/鲁棒性/内存安全）、 各库错误处理与内存管理 API、以及编译/测试命令。用于给数据库写 C/C++ 原生函数、 扩展 SQL 能力时。知识提炼自 SIGMOD 2026 DBCooker/OpenCook 论文与源码。 ([aresbit/db-kernel-func-skill](https://github.com/aresbit/db-kernel-func-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **db-keys**: Audit primary-key strategy — missing PK (severity 5), surrogate vs natural keys, UUIDv4 index fragmentation vs time-ordered UUIDv7/ULID/bigint, and int4/serial exhaustion on high-volume tables. Module M2. Feeds both the Design & Integrity (Llaves) and Performance & Scale (Escala) scores. ([Hainrixz/claude-db](https://github.com/Hainrixz/claude-db/tree/HEAD/skills/db-keys) · ★24 · 2 copies)
- **db-lab**: Optional project-aware database learning lab using actual schema, ORM, migration, query, and query-plan evidence for SQL, relational design, debugging, change exploration, and interview practice. ([mriekki/project-forge-platform](https://github.com/mriekki/project-forge-platform/tree/HEAD/assets/generated/core/core/skills/db-lab) · ★1)
- **db-learn**: Entry point for the continuous-learning loop - runs failure-learner, knowledge-promotion (with db-eval regression gate), drift-monitor and the knowledge-gaps report in order. Use on a schedule (e.g. nightly) or when the user asks the bot to learn from recent feedback. - **db-lock-analyzer**: 数据库锁分析与死锁检测，支持当前锁分析、死锁检测、锁等待链追踪、锁统计报告、事务终止。 使用场景： - 用户说"看锁" -&gt; 执行 analyze - 用户说"死锁" -&gt; 执行 deadlocks - 用户说"阻塞" -&gt; 执行 chains - 用户说"锁报告" -&gt; 执行 report - 用户说"终止事务" -&gt; 执行 kill &lt;事务ID&gt; 用法： - python -m dbskiter --output-mode=ai --database=&lt;name&gt; lock analyze - python -m dbskiter --output-mode=ai --database=&lt;name&gt; lock deadlocks - python -m dbskiter --output-mode=ai --database=&lt;name&gt; lock chains - python -m dbskiter --output-mode=ai --database=&lt;name&gt; lock report - python -m dbskiter --output-mode=ai --database=&lt;name&gt; lock kill &lt;transaction_id&gt; ([magicCzc/dbskiter](https://github.com/magicCzc/dbskiter/tree/HEAD/.trae/skills/db-lock-analyzer) · ★10)
- **db-lock-audit**: Detecta las operaciones de migración que bloquean la tabla o la reescriben: cambio de tipo, columna NOT NULL con DEFAULT, CREATE INDEX sin CONCURRENTLY, RENAME en un paso y VACUUM FULL. Úsala cuando una migración pueda dejar la tabla bloqueada o reescribirla durante minutos. English: flag the migration operations that lock or rewrite a table and give the safe alternative. "백업 해줘·백업 되고 있어?·DB 가 너무 커·용량·디스크·오래된 대화 정리·데이터 옮기기·복구·날아가면" 류 요청, 그리고 데이터가 걸린 유지보수를 시작하기 전에 사용한다. ★백업은 v0.27.0 부터 **자동**이다(스케줄이 아니라 데몬 유지보수 루프) — 스케줄 목록에 없다고 '백업이 없다'고 결론내지 말고 `/status` 나 `&lt;home&gt;/data/backup/` 을 보라. 정리 ≠ 삭제: 콜드 레코드는 옮기되 지우지 않는다. TRIGGER when 엔티티 필드/테이블/제약 추가·수정·삭제, ALTER/CREATE/DROP TABLE 의도, "컬럼 추가/스키마 바꿔/테이블 만들어/FK 걸자/인덱스 추가" 발화, db/migration/ 아래 파일 작성 요청.
